22       ! /************************************************************************************************************************
22       ! *******************
23         
24         Project: Workers Comp WPC
25         Date: March 2023
26         Author: Matt Walkowiak
27         Description:
28         	- this program builds the modeling dataset for the Workers Comp WPC model
29         	- gathers WC claims with closed dates from 1/1/2018 to 12/31/2022
30          	- each record in the data represents one claim
31         	- the main query below uses the CLM table in CCDS as the starting point,
32         		and then appends on other tables as needed
33         	- the y variable I am trying to predict is "number of touches", defined as:
34         		number of touches = activities + documents + notes
35         	- the only external data I append is a Middle Market indicator,
36         		all other data is confined to CCDS
37         	- WC adjusters work remotely and do not travel (unless to meet with agents),
38         		so I don't have to incorporate drive time in the model
39         
40         
41         *************************************************************************************************************************
41       ! ******************/
42         /* UPDATE - month, day, year */
43         /*%let date=05292024;*/
44         
45         /*INITIALIZE ACCESS TO CCDS*/
46         LIBNAME CCDS ORACLE PATH=CDEP1 SCHEMA=BRFADM Authdomain=Oracle_Auth_CCDS;
NOTE:  Credential obtained from SAS metadata server.
NOTE: Libref CCDS was successfully assigned as follows: 
      Engine:        ORACLE 
      Physical Name: CDEP1
47         libname WCWH '/ere/warehouse/workerscompensation';
NOTE: Libref WCWH was successfully assigned as follows: 
      Engine:        V9 
      Physical Name: /ere/warehouse/workerscompensation

49         /*%let flag=0;*/
50         /*%let output_path_csv=/ere/local/shared/actuarial/Share/Claims/WPC/Workers Comp/2026/02-18-2026;*/
51         
52         
53         /* Project library to save data */
54         /*libname WC '/ere/local/shared/actuarial/Share/Claims/WPC/TESTING 2/Workers Comp/2024/07-26-2024';*/
55         
56         /* Dates for closed claims */
57         /*%LET from_dt = '01JANUARY2019';*/
58         /*%LET to_dt = '31DECEMBER2022';*/

60         /* Import Middle Market accounts */
61         /*proc import out=mm_acct*/
62         /*    datafile="/ere/local/shared/actuarial/Modeling/Claims/Resource Capacity/Workers Comp Adjuster/Data/WC MM Accounts -
62       !  Reformatted.xlsx"*/
63         /*    dbms=xlsx*/
64         /*    replace;*/
65         /*    getnames=YES;*/
66         /*run;*/

68         PROC SQL;
69            CREATE TABLE mm_acct AS
70            SELECT
71         		DISTINCT(t1.PolicyNumber) AS Attribute_Name,
72         		t1.MiddleMarketIndicator AS Attribute_Description
73            FROM WCWH.WC t1;

392        		AND INCID.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
393        		AND INCID.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
394        
395        		/* CLAIMANT DATA- new- no dups using this data- expand */
396        	LEFT JOIN BRFADM.CNTC ON C.CLMT_ID = CNTC.CNTC_NTRL_ID
397        	    AND CNTC.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
398        	    AND CNTC.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
399        
400        		/* POL TABLE */
401        		/* Need this to join on MM indicator in Step 5 */
402        	LEFT JOIN BRFADM.POL ON C.POL_NTRL_ID = POL.POL_NTRL_ID
403        
404        	    AND POL.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
405        	    AND POL.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
406        
407        /* RESTRICTIONS */
408            Where
409                C.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
410                and C.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
411        		and C.LOB_DC = 'WorkersCompensationLine'
412        
413            Order By
414                C.CLM_NR
415        );

431        Proc SQL;
432        CONNECT USING CCDS;
433        Create table body_part as
434        Select * from connection to CCDS
435        (
436            Select distinct
437        		C.LOB_DC
438        		,C.CLM_NR
439        		,INCID.CLM_NTRL_ID
440        		,INCID.INCID_NTRL_ID
441        		,INCID.gnrl_inj_typ_cd
442        		,BODY_PART_DTL.PRIM_BODY_PART_TYP_DC
443        
444            From BRFADM.CLM C
445        
446        	LEFT JOIN BRFADM.INCID ON C.CLM_NTRL_ID = INCID.CLM_NTRL_ID
447        		AND INCID.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
448        		AND INCID.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
449        
450        	LEFT JOIN BRFADM.BODY_PART_DTL ON INCID.INCID_NTRL_ID = BODY_PART_DTL.INCID_NTRL_ID
451        	    AND BODY_PART_DTL.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
452        	    AND BODY_PART_DTL.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'	
453        
454            Where
455                C.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
456                and C.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
457        		and C.LOB_DC = 'WorkersCompensationLine'
458            Order By
459                C.CLM_NR
460        );

577        /******* Step 4: Count number of activities associated with each claim ***************/
578        
579        Proc SQL;
580        CONNECT USING CCDS;
581        Create table act as
582        Select * from connection to CCDS
583        (
584            Select Distinct
585        		C.LOB_DC
586        		,C.CLM_NR
587        		,C.CRT_DT
588        		,C.CLS_DT
589        		,A.ACT_NTRL_ID
590        		,A.ASGN_USR_ID
591        		,A.ASGN_BY_USR_ID
592                ,A.ASGN_DT
593                ,A.TRGT_DT
594                ,A.ACT_CLS_DT
595                ,A.ACT_STT_DC
596        		,A.ACT_SUBJ_DC
597                ,A.ACT_TYP_DC
598                ,A.ACT_CLASS_DC
599                ,A.ACT_DC
600                ,A.SBTYP_DC
601        		,UTT.USR_TTL as Asgn_To_Ttl
602                ,G.GRP_NM as Team
603                ,AP.ACT_PTTRN_DC
604                ,AP.ACT_PTTRN_CD
605                ,AP.ACT_CAT_DC
606            From
607                BRFADM.CLM C
608        
609                Left Join BRFADM.ACT A on C.CLM_NTRL_ID = A.CLM_NTRL_ID and A.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y' and A.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
610         		Left Join BRFADM.ACT_PTTRN AP on A.ACT_PTTRN_NTRL_ID = AP.ACT_PTTRN_NTRL_ID and AP.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y' and AP.REC_RET_IN
610      !  = 'N'
611                Left Join BRFADM.USR U on A.ASGN_USR_ID = U.USR_NTRL_ID and U.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y' and U.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
612                Left Join BRFADM.USR_TTL_TMP UTT on U.USR_NTRL_ID = UTT.USR_TTL_ID and A.ASGN_DT between UTT.USR_TTL_STRT_DT and
612      ! UTT.USR_TTL_END_DT
613                Left Join BRFADM.GRP G on A.ASGN_GRP_ID = G.GRP_NTRL_ID and G.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y' and G.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
614            Where
615                C.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
616                and C.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
617        		and C.LOB_DC = 'WorkersCompensationLine'
618        /*		and c.CLM_NR = '000100019728'*/
619            Order By
620                C.CLM_NR
621        );
NOTE: The data value for column ACT_SUBJ_DC (ACT_SUBJ_DC) was truncated or was out of range 98 times when retrieving that data from 
      the DBMS.

642        /******* Step 5: Count number of touches associated with each claim ***************/
643        
644        Proc Sql;
645        CONNECT USING CCDS;
646        create table touches_count as
647        Select * from connection to CCDS
648        (
649             Select
650                     C.CLM_NTRL_ID
651                   ,C.CLM_NR
652        		   ,C.JURIS_STATE_DC
653                   ,Count(Distinct H.HIST_NTRL_ID) as Total_Touches
654                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC = 'Viewed' Then 1 Else 0 End) as Views
655                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC = 'Assigned' Then 1 Else 0 End) as Asgn
656                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC = 'Closed' Then 1 Else 0 End) as Closes
657                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC = 'Custom' Then 1 Else 0 End) as Custom_Evnts
658                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC = 'Delayed' Then 1 Else 0 End) as Delays
659                   ,SUM(Case When H.HIST_TYP_DC not in ('Viewed','Assigned','Closed','Custom','Delayed') Then 1 Else 0 End) as
659      ! All_Other
660             From
661                   BRFADM.CLM C
662                   Left Join BRFADM.HIST H on C.CLM_NTRL_ID = H.CLM_NTRL_ID
663                   Left Join BRFADM.USR U on H.USR_NTRL_ID = U.USR_NTRL_ID and U.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y' and U.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
664                   Left Join BRFADM.USR_TTL_TMP UTT on U.USR_NTRL_ID = UTT.USR_TTL_ID and H.EVNT_DT between UTT.USR_TTL_STRT_DT
664      ! and UTT.USR_TTL_END_DT
665             Where
666                   C.CRNT_REC_IN = 'Y'
667                   and C.REC_RET_IN = 'N'
668        		   and C.LOB_DC = 'WorkersCompensationLine'
669                   and UTT.USR_TTL in ('Sr Workers Comp Adjuster', 'WORKERS COMP ADJUSTER I', 'WORKERS COMP SPECIALIST', 'Workers
669      !  Compensation Specialist',
670        								'Workers Compensation Adjuster' , 'Workers Comp Adjuster I', 'WORKERS COMPENSATION SPECIALIST', 'Workers Comp
670      ! Adjuster II',
671        								'SR WORKERS COMP ADJUSTER', 'Workers Compensation Adjuster II', 'Workers Comp Specialist', 'Workers Compensation
671      ! Adjuster I',
672        								'Workers Comp Adjuser II', 'Sr Workers Compensation Adjuster', 'WORKERS COMP ADJUSTER II', 'Workers Comp
672      ! Adjuster')
673             Group By
674                     C.CLM_NTRL_ID
675                   ,C.CLM_NR
676        		   ,C.JURIS_STATE_DC
677        );
NOTE: Table WORK.TOUCHES_COUNT created, with 190919 rows and 10 columns.
